#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_config.py:539` — unchecked Pydantic/dispatch paths and the lexical scan still admit profile/config cross-pairs
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: the selected profile governs where clinical assertions are written and which populated sections may be treated as intentionally unmapped. The round-11 relational shape closes honest cross-pair construction, but the generation boundary still accepts forgeable Pydantic evidence and providers still accept a raw request. A future Phase-6 implementation can therefore bypass membership while remaining valid Python and, for several forms, passing mypy and the new scan.
- Current behaviour: `build_note_request` dynamically reads the incoming object's overridable `binding.template_profile` and `binding.config_digest` properties (`note_config.py:649-653`) without revalidating/canonicalising the binding. Pydantic's `model_construct` and `model_copy(update=...)` skip validators, and a type-correct subclass can override either derived property; all three forms produced `template_profile_id="profile-a"` with config B's digest in the no-write probe and reached `ExtractiveNoteProvider.generate_sections`. Independently, `NoteRequest.model_validate(...)` accepts any regex-valid profile id/digest pair and the providers accept it (`note.py:542-582,1029,1164`). The guard at `test_note_config.py:423-440` searches only `(?<![\w.])NoteRequest\(`, excludes all `note.py` basenames, and never guards `_assemble_note_request`, so representative bypasses are invisible to it. Removing `NoteRequest` from `__all__` affects star-import only; it does not prevent explicit imports or Pydantic classmethods.
- Desired behaviour: make the one generation boundary re-establish the relation at use time from non-overridable validated field data, rather than trusting virtual properties on an incoming model instance, and make the shipping-source policy cover every raw construction/assembly route. A suitable fix may accept the source `NoteConfig` plus selected id and bind inside the builder, or canonicalise/revalidate into the exact base binding type before deriving locally; pair this with `final`/revalidation posture as appropriate. Replace the regex convention with an AST/symbol guard (or a stronger provider-facing type boundary) that whitelists only the exact internal `NoteRequest` assembly site and the exact `_assemble_note_request` caller, while scanning all package modules including `note.py` and Pydantic constructor classmethods.
- Pattern to follow: the existing `TemplateProfile._check_profile` / `NoteConfig._check_config` relational validators, but re-run or reconstruct at the trust boundary rather than assuming every Pydantic instance arrived through validating `__init__`; keep the raw assembler's allow-list as narrow and explicit as the logging tripwire's derived-field allow-list.
- Pattern siblings: `BoundTemplateProfile` subclassing and `model_construct`; unchecked `model_copy(update=...)`; `NoteRequest.model_validate/model_validate_json/model_construct/model_copy`; qualified/aliased raw constructors; `_assemble_note_request` callers; the whole-file `note.py` scan exclusion.
- Invariant: every `NoteRequest` accepted by a shipping provider carries a template profile id that is a member of the exact validated resolved `NoteConfig` whose locally derived digest the request records; no raw Pydantic or assembler path in shipping source can bypass that relation.
- Verification: add red/green cases for a binding subclass overriding `config_digest`, `BoundTemplateProfile.model_construct` with invalid/unchecked source data, unchecked `model_copy(update=...)`, and raw `NoteRequest.model_validate`; each must fail before provider invocation. Add policy-test fixtures or AST assertions proving a future module is rejected for bare, qualified/aliased, and Pydantic-classmethod request construction and for calling `_assemble_note_request`, while the one internal assembly site and test fixtures remain allowed. Retain normal constructor/JSON round-trips, sole-profile auto-bind, explicit multi-profile selection, unknown/zero-profile rejection, nested immutability, and run ruff, mypy strict, and the full pytest suite.
- Regression risk: over-broad source scanning can flag annotations, comments, test fixtures, or the legitimate internal call; an over-broad runtime type check can reject supported subclasses without a clear error. Keep the allow-list syntax-aware and exact, return a typed fail-closed boundary error, and preserve `NoteRequest` importability for annotations without treating importability as construction authority.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — this is the same Task 3.1 / rounds 10–11 generation invariant, tested against the post-fix claim that cross-pairs are unrepresentable through any construction of the binding and that shipping construction is scan-guarded.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Executor re-verified all four peer claims against the code before fixing (no separate leg-1 ran; the builder's property reads, the pydantic escape-hatch semantics, the provider's acceptance of any `NoteRequest`, and every named regex gap — including the whole-basename `note.py` exclusion that would have exempted Phase 7's planned `ui/note.py` — all confirmed). Fix, per the finding's Desired shape: (1) **the boundary re-establishes the relation at use time** — `build_note_request(document, config, template_profile_id=None, ...)` now accepts the source config + selected id, canonicalises via new `_canonical_config` (round-trip through `NoteConfig.__pydantic_serializer__.to_json` — BASE-class schema, field data only, no subclass method resolution — then full `model_validate_json` re-validation, failing closed as `NoteConfigInvalidError`), binds internally, and derives the digest locally from the canonical config; no caller-supplied wrapper is trusted. `bind_template_profile` canonicalises identically, so UI-facing binding evidence also derives from validated field data. (2) **`@final` posture** on `NoteRequest` and `BoundTemplateProfile` (shipping-source subclassing is now a mypy-strict error; runtime subclassing stays irrelevant to the boundary). (3) **AST/symbol guard replaces the regex** (`TestConstructionGuard`): scans EVERY package module including `note.py`, allow-lists exactly two (module, function, symbol) call sites, and flags bare/qualified/aliased/multiline `NoteRequest` calls, its four pydantic constructor classmethods reached through the class, `NoteRequest`/`BoundTemplateProfile` subclass definitions, guarded-symbol aliasing and import-renaming, and `_assemble_note_request` imports/calls outside `note_config.py` — 15 parametrized bypass fixtures prove each route flags, an annotation-use fixture proves importability is preserved, and a `ui/note.py` fixture proves the basename hole is closed (allow-list keys on package-relative path). Pre-fix DEMONSTRATED per contract: transient probes drove a property-overriding binding subclass AND a `model_construct` forgery with an unchecked duck source to `generate_sections`, both carrying A's profile with B's digest; probes reverted. Post-fix red/green: lying-`NoteConfig`-subclass digest ignored (canonical derivation), `model_construct` and `model_copy(update=...)` forgeries and duck configs fail closed BEFORE provider invocation, sole-profile auto-bind / unknown-id / zero-profile / cross-pair / JSON round-trip behaviour all retained. HONESTY BOUNDARY stated in-code (builder + `NoteRequest` docstrings): value-typed escape hatches (`request.model_copy` through a variable), monkey-patching, and `__dict__` tampering are statically invisible and outside the same-user threat model — claimed as guarded-by-test vs type-enforced vs out-of-scope, per mechanism. Tests migrated (`test_note.py` `_request`/merged-cluster site → `(document, _TEST_CONFIG, ...)`; `_BINDING` removed). Verified: ruff clean / mypy strict 26 files / pytest **877 passed**.

#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/tests/test_note_config.py:504` — the AST guard enumerates constructor spellings instead of confining runtime `NoteRequest` references
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now

- Why it matters: the use-time builder is sound only if shipping code cannot hand a raw request to a provider. A future Phase-6 caller can currently use ordinary, public validation machinery to create a request with an invented profile id/digest and pass the entire suite's source-policy guard, so the Task-3.1 generation invariant still depends on which constructor spelling a caller happens to choose.

- Pattern siblings: `test_note_config.py:521-600` (`_name_of`, call/alias/subclass visitors); `test_note_config.py:620-671` (15 fixtures omit current Pydantic/adapter and class-value routes); `note.py:543-563` and `note_config.py:674-692` (mechanism claims); `note.py:610-627` (stale `BoundTemplateProfile` boundary description).
- Invariant: every request accepted by a shipping provider was built through `note_config.build_note_request` from one canonical, fully re-validated `NoteConfig`; raw request construction is absent from every package module except the single internal assembly call, independent of Pydantic API spelling or simple class-value indirection.
- Verification: add red/green guard fixtures for `model_validate_strings`, `TypeAdapter(NoteRequest)`, supported legacy constructors, `__pydantic_validator__`, walrus/container/default-argument aliases, and a second call inside each allow-listed function; prove annotation-only imports and references remain accepted; assert exactly one permitted `NoteRequest` call and one permitted assembler call across the package; retain the runtime canonicalisation attacks and run ruff, mypy strict, and the full pytest suite.

NO pydantic API list remains anywhere in the guard. The permitted surface is pinned by NODE COUNT package-wide (`NoteRequest`: 1 call in `note._assemble_note_request`; `BoundTemplateProfile`: 2 calls in `bind_template_profile`; assembler: 1 call in `build_note_request`) and asserted exactly in the clean-package test, so a new spelling OR a second call inside an allow-listed function fails by default (a dedicated test shows the two-call case is counted, not hidden). #### PR-HIGH-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_config.py:328` — list shape is treated as proof of atomicity, so claims can collapse or explode
- Classification: Pre-existing
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: the clinical-safety contract is explicit per-assertion confirmation. If one list entry contains two independently confirmable claims, one click confirms both; if the same entry appears twice, one exact claim becomes two separately confirmable assertions and can be stored twice. The proposal-only boundary still holds, but the confirmation unit no longer equals the clinical-assertion unit it is meant to evidence.
- Current behaviour: `_refuse_prose_blob` rejects only when the OUTER field value is a bare `str`/`bytes`; `_AssertionText` validates only text shape/bounds, and neither `AutofillRule` nor `PrefillTemplate` checks entry atomicity or duplicates. Consequently `expansion=["Rest advised. Ice pack use explained."]` and the analogous one-entry prefill seed validate, then `note_fill.py:173-190` / `:284-300` emit one proposal for that two-claim blob. Repeating the exact entry twice also validates; the positional `entry_index` gives the copies different proposal IDs, and the probe produced `count=2, unique_texts=1` for both emitters. This directly falsifies the helper docstring's claim that a bare-string container is "the one place" per-assertion can become per-blob.
- Desired behaviour: fail closed at the CONFIG AUTHORING boundary for mechanically identifiable compound prose and exact duplicate assertions, with the same fix-naming guidance that sends the clinician back to one entry per claim. Because arbitrary clinical prose cannot be semantically decomposed safely, define an explicit authoring-time control/assumption for cases beyond conservative mechanical checks; do not split, join, or silently deduplicate clinical text at runtime.
- Pattern to follow: the existing duplicate-id/normalised-trigger validators in `NoteConfig._check_config` — reject invalid authored structure before it reaches an emitter, and keep `note_fill.py` as the deliberately inference-free one-entry → one-proposal mapper.
- Pattern siblings: `AutofillRule.expansion` duplicate/compound entries; `PrefillTemplate.seed_assertions` duplicate `(section_key, seed_text)` and compound `seed_text` entries; identical section/text expansions across multiple simultaneously matched rules should be dispositioned by the same authored-identity policy rather than left to runtime accident.
- Invariant: for every matched rule or selected prefill, logical atomic assertions and emitted proposal IDs are one-to-one — no proposal contains more than one independently confirmable claim, and no exact authored assertion yields multiple proposals.
- Verification: add model-level and `load_note_config` red/green tests for a one-element, two-claim expansion and seed; exact duplicate expansion and seed entries; and any chosen cross-rule duplicate policy. Retain the distinct-three-entry → three-proposal tests and add a regression that proves the emitter performs no runtime splitting/deduplication. Run ruff, mypy strict, and the full pytest suite.
- Regression risk: an aggressive punctuation/conjunction heuristic can reject valid atomic clinical wording or become forbidden semantic parsing under another name. Keep mechanical checks conservative, make ambiguous authoring fail with actionable guidance or explicit review, and preserve exact shown wording/digest behaviour.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 4.0 owns atomic-assertion authoring, Tasks 4.1–4.2 promise one proposal per atomic assertion, and the review brief explicitly asks whether prose can be smuggled or one claim can explode.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Practitioner disposition: fix now, both directions, conservative shape (leg-1 recommendation). Files changed: `note_config.py` (lexical `_looks_compound` guard `[.!?;]\s+\S` + fix-naming messages; `SingleClaimEntry` explicit per-entry override for expansions — `Literal[True]`, author must write it; `single_claim: bool = False` flag on `PrefillSeedAssertion`; normalised duplicate rejection via `content_tokens` for expansion entries and `(section_key, seed_text)` seed pairs; `AutofillRule.expansion_texts()` unwrap helper; `_refuse_prose_blob`'s falsified "the one place" docstring reworded), `note_fill.py` (consumes `expansion_texts()`; module docstring now states the enforcement and NAMES the residue: conjunction-joined compounds and false overrides are authoring-time human error outside any mechanical check — no runtime split/join/dedup anywhere). Verified: RED demonstrated in-repo (6 rejection tests failed pre-fix: two-claim entry ×2 models + loader, exact + normalised duplicates ×2 models) then GREEN; ordinary atomic entries incl. trailing-terminator and decimal "1.5 mg" pinned ACCEPTED; override path pinned accepted incl. canonical round-trip (digest-stable); override does NOT bypass the duplicate check; emitter pinned split/dedup-free one-to-one in both autofill and prefill. Sibling sites: expansion duplicates/compound ✓, seed duplicates/compound ✓; cross-rule identical expansions dispositioned ALLOWED deliberately — two different triggers may legitimately expand to the same advice text, each firing is evidence of its own trigger with its own rule id and per-proposal confirmation (recorded here as the explicit authored-identity policy the peer asked for). Suites: ruff clean, mypy strict 27 files, pytest 913 → **931 passed**.
- /fix date: 2026-08-10
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_config.py:365` — a compact semicolon compound bypasses the guard and the stated residue
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: a terse but valid authoring form such as `"Rest advised;ice pack use explained."` contains two independently confirmable clinical claims yet becomes one proposal and one confirmation. That is the same confirmation-unit/cardinality failure Round 15 fixed, on a mechanically identifiable separator that does not require semantic parsing.
- Current behaviour: `_SENTENCE_BREAK_RE = re.compile(r"[.!?;]\s+\S")` requires whitespace after every terminator, so a semicolon followed immediately by the next claim validates in both `AutofillRule` and `PrefillSeedAssertion`; canonicalisation preserves it and each emitter maps it to one proposal. The residue comments in `note_config.py:361-364` and `note_fill.py:24-27` say the remaining unchecked compounds lack terminator punctuation (or use a false override), so this accepted punctuated form is not honestly named.
- Desired behaviour: keep period handling conservative for decimals/abbreviations, but treat an internal semicolon as the compound signal whether or not whitespace follows, still requiring real text after it and still allowing the explicit per-entry override. Reword the residue to claim only what the resulting lexical shape enforces.
- Pattern to follow: keep the one shared authoring-boundary helper and make its punctuation alternatives explicit, e.g. period/exclamation/question plus whitespace, OR semicolon plus optional whitespace, followed by content; do not add runtime splitting or an abbreviation allow-list.
- Pattern siblings: `AutofillRule._check_expansion_atomicity` and `PrefillSeedAssertion._check_atomicity` both consume the helper and close together; update the residue in the `note_config.py` comment and `note_fill.py` module docstring, plus both model-side regression fixtures. No second matcher should be added.
- Invariant: every mechanically identifiable semicolon-separated pair of clinical claims is refused at authoring unless that exact entry carries an explicit `single_claim` statement; no emitter infers claim boundaries.
- Verification: add red/green model tests for `"Rest advised;ice pack use explained."` in autofill and prefill, including loader propagation of the fix-naming message; retain semicolon-plus-space rejection, trailing-semicolon acceptance, decimal/range acceptance, abbreviation override, duplicate rejection with overrides, forged-config refusal, and the split/join/dedup-free emitter pins. Run ruff, mypy strict, and the full pytest suite.
- Regression risk: compact semicolon notation that is genuinely one assertion will begin failing loudly; this is the safe direction and the existing per-entry override is the intended resolution. A careless all-punctuation `\s*` rewrite could falsely flag decimals or repeated trailing punctuation, so constrain the no-whitespace branch to semicolon.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 4.0 owns entry atomicity and the review brief explicitly asks whether semicolon-joined compounds false-accept.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Practitioner disposition (2026-08-11, raised effort xhigh): **Option B — design reframe, not a patch.** The deny-list guard (`_SENTENCE_BREAK_RE`/`_looks_compound`) is REMOVED and replaced by a conservative single-claim ALLOW-LIST, `_is_atomic_shape` + `_abbreviation_chain_dot` (`note_config.py`) — an entry is accepted as atomic only when EVERY character matches the documented shape (letters/digits/spaces; in-claim punctuation; positional rules: hyphen between alphanumerics, other dashes between digits only, `.` between digits or in ≥2-pair letter-dot chains not followed by a Capitalised word, `:` between digits, trailing terminator runs incl. fullwidth forms); anything else — including separators never enumerated — fails CLOSED into the fix-naming `_atomicity_message` or the explicit per-entry `single_claim` override. ONE shared helper, both consumers (`AutofillRule._check_expansion_atomicity`, `PrefillSeedAssertion._check_atomicity`); duplicate rejection untouched; override still `Literal[True]`, entry-local; NO runtime splitting (emitter pins green); NO word-list of abbreviations (the chain rule is positional; the smuggle route "q.d. Rest advised" is pinned refused via the Capital-continuation refusal). Semantic-surface lesson cited in the code comment. RED demonstrated in-repo: 5 leak-set tests failed pre-fix — `A;B` (both models), `advised.Ice`, and NOVEL colon-join and spaced-em-dash joins (the class-closure proof) — then GREEN. Acceptance extended and pinned: hyphen ranges/compounds, en-dash digit ranges, `14:30`, `q.i.d.` terminal AND mid-sentence into lowercase, commas/measurements; `Dr. Smith` refuses into the override BY DESIGN (mechanically indistinguishable from a sentence end — stated, per the contract's "state which"). Honesty: residue comments in both modules now name the exact surviving surface — comma-splice/conjunction joins inside the allowed alphabet and override misuse. `import re` dropped from `note_config.py` (no longer used). Suites: ruff clean, mypy strict 27 files, pytest 931 → **949 passed**.

- Why it matters: `"Paracetamol q.i.d. ice applied."` contains a medication-frequency claim and a treatment-performed claim that must be independently confirmable, but it validates as one autofill expansion or one prefill seed and therefore becomes one proposal/confirmation. - Current behaviour: `_abbreviation_chain_dot()` returns true for a chain-final dot followed by a space whenever the next character is lowercase or decimal (`note_config.py:412-415`). `_is_atomic_shape()` therefore accepts the example; both model validators consume that helper, canonicalisation preserves it, and the emitters map the one entry to one proposal. A narrow patch that rejects only the reproduced word (`ice`) or only lowercase letters would recreate the deny-list failure class.

Part 1 (strict narrowing): `_abbreviation_chain_dot` no longer allows a chain-final dot followed by ANY text — the continuation branch is DELETED, so the helper now recognises interior chain dots only (dot-letter-dot) and a chain's final dot passes solely through `_is_atomic_shape`'s trailing-terminator branch, i.e. the chain must END the entry. Removes an acceptance route, adds none. Part 2 (derived residue): the residue comments in `note_config.py` and `note_fill.py` are rewritten as an explicit list DERIVED from the allow-list's permissive positions — comma/conjunction joins, hyphen-between-alphanumerics joins, digit-boundary joins (each mechanically inseparable from the `mid-back`/`X-ray`/`1.5mg` wording its position admits), plus override misuse — accepted by design with Phase 7 confirmation UI + Phase 5 checking named as the compensating controls; no prose "only X survives" claim remains (three falsifications in three rounds prompted the derived form).   - LOW-001: `transcription.py:542` — the cepstral-mean-normalisation comment claimed the gain constant is removed "exactly" and that the centroid is "already gain-invariant"; both are approximate, because of the `1e-10` stabilisers in `np.log(mel_power + 1e-10)` and in the centroid denominator. The round's own pre-fix run had already measured the centroid residue at `1.7e-4` over 20 dB. Comment now states the bound, why it is not binding for real audio (int16 quantisation noise alone puts a mel band near `1e-8`, two orders above the floor, and digital silence never reaches the function), and the measured centroid figure — decision: Applied
  - LOW-002: `note.py:884` — `SpeakerRolePreselection`'s docstring said a preselection is withheld on "an exact tie" while the code applies `_ROLE_TIE_EPSILON`; the docstring now says within-epsilon and names the constant — decision: Applied

Both removed, with the reason recorded where the reader would otherwise wonder — decision: Applied
- **Dropped on verification (logged so the next reviewer does not re-derive them):**
  - *Candidate: CMN could make a silent or flat-spectrum segment indistinguishable from a voice.* Dropped — silence maps to the zero vector while any real voice maps to its non-zero demeaned shape, so the two stay separable; and VAD emits only speech, with both call sites refusing empty PCM before this function is reached.
  - *Candidate: `note.py` has passed ~1450 lines and `MockNoteModelProvider` is test-only code shipping in a production module.* Dropped as a finding for THIS round — the condition was created by Phase 1, not by this task's 161 added lines, and six prior rounds including four cross-family peer rounds passed on it. #### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note.py:1397` — `dose_change` still accepts any number change as its named dosage class
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: Task 5.0 exists because a mock behaviour that returns different output for the wrong reason makes the safety matrix look stronger than it is. The same four-round class survives for dosage: the provider and independent oracle both accept changing a pain score, duration, date, or measurement even when no medication dose was changed.
- Current behaviour: `_change_dose` doubles the first digit run anywhere in any assertion. `_oracle_dose_change` at `desktop/tests/test_note.py:1382` proves only that one numeric token became another numeric token; it does not require a medication/dose anchor. The default oracle fixture therefore passes after changing `3 out of ten` to `6 out of ten`, and both matrix dose cells change non-dose numbers (`3 out of ten`; `20 minutes`) while reporting `reconstruction_mismatch` as if the named dosage class had been exercised.
- Desired behaviour: `dose_change` must mutate an explicitly dose-shaped medication quantity and otherwise raise `NoteProviderError`; its test-side oracle must independently prove the changed number is the quantity bound to a medication dose, not merely numeric.
- Pattern to follow: the Task-5.0 injection repair — define the provider's fixture applicability contract, keep an independently authored test-side postcondition, and make inexpressible fixtures raise. Do not reuse the provider's comparator or the production checker's parser as the oracle.
- Pattern siblings: `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py:658` and `:717` (`ab-dose` / `ab2-dose`); the generic no-collapse test at `desktop/tests/test_note.py:1170` remains only a difference backstop and must not substitute for the named oracle.
- Invariant: every non-`faithful` behaviour either produces its specifically named Axis-B class or raises because the fixture cannot express that class.
- Verification: add red/green cases where a pain score/duration precedes a real medication dose and the dose — not the earlier number — changes; measurement-only/date-only fixtures must raise; require the independent oracle to reject a numeric mutation with no medication-dose relation; update the two matrix cells to exact dose-shaped fixtures or raise cells.
- Regression risk: narrowing the mock's applicability will turn existing loosely numeric fixtures into loud failures; update every registered dose cell together so the matrix cannot lose coverage silently.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.0 explicitly owns named-class postconditions and the user requested the recurring class be attacked.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: RED demonstrated in-repo first — `test_dose_change_without_a_medication_anchored_dose_fails_loudly` (pain score + duration fixture) FAILED pre-fix because the mock happily doubled the pain score. Fix follows the Task-5.0 injection-repair shape exactly: `note.py` gained the dose FIXTURE CONTRACT as instrument constants (`_DOSE_MEDICATIONS` / `_DOSE_MARKERS`, the `_INJECTION_VERBS` precedent — closed authoring vocabularies, failure direction safe, "daily" is a marker while "days" deliberately is not), and `_change_dose` now mutates ONLY a quantity sitting between a medication token and a unit/regimen marker, trying every dose site (zero doubles to itself and the scan moves on — round-4 semantics preserved) and returning None otherwise so `_mutate` raises the updated `_MUTATION_REQUIREMENT` message. Test side: `_oracle_dose_change` now proves the changed number is MEDICATION-BOUND with the test's OWN `_ORACLE_DOSE_MEDICATIONS`/`_ORACLE_DOSE_MARKERS` vocabulary (never the provider's constants, never the checker's parser); `_behaviour_request` gives `dose_change` its own dose-shaped fixture (`DOSE_INSTRUCTION`) since the default document deliberately has no medication; the zero-dose / leading-zero / no-op-scan fixtures became med-anchored so their original points survive. Pattern siblings both updated in the SAME change: `ab-dose` now provably mutates the genuine dose in "Take paracetamol 500 twice daily." (pinned by the PR-MED-003 statement declaration), and `ab2-dose` became raise-cell `ab2-dose-no-medication` (its only number is a walking duration). Verified: red → green, full suite 1096 at this fix's close, final 1102.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-002: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:414` — adjacency still binds an unrelated temporal number into a blocking dose contradiction
- Classification: Fix-induced
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: `contradiction` is an error that will block write, copy, and Complete. The round-19 narrowing still produces the same false-blocking class whenever the unrelated number is immediately after the medication, a normal form in medication history such as “stopped paracetamol 2 days ago.”
- Current behaviour: after a medication token, `_claims_from_tokens` accepts the first numeric candidate when `offset == 0` even without a dose unit or regimen evidence. An in-memory reproduction with faithfully quoted `I stopped paracetamol 2 days ago` and confirmed `Paracetamol 500 advised.` emits `contradiction`: the shared anchor is real, but the quoted value `2` is a time interval, not a dose.
- Desired behaviour: immediate adjacency alone must not convert a clearly temporal/count/measurement quantity into a dose. Only text carrying sufficiently explicit dose structure should compare; ambiguous adjacent forms should follow the module's stated fail-toward-silence rule and remain carried by confirmation.
- Pattern to follow: the existing unit-marked branch and the plan's matched-entity discipline: establish the value's relation to the medication, not merely its distance from the token. Keep the recogniser narrow rather than adding a broad semantic claim.
- Pattern siblings: adjacent bare forms in both transcript and clinician-authored assertions; temporal units (`days`, `weeks`), visit/count language, and measurements immediately after a medication name. Recheck the residue compound and true `paracetamol 500 twice daily` controls while narrowing.
- Invariant: unrelated numbers, medications, measurements, or body-region claims must never manufacture a blocking contradiction; anchor matching includes the value-to-anchor relation, not token adjacency alone.
- Verification: add the exact `paracetamol 2 days ago` red/green regression plus representative adjacent count/measurement negatives; retain true adjacent and unit-marked dose positives, multi-medication zero-error fixtures, and low-confidence grading tests.
- Regression risk: an over-narrow fix can silence terse but genuine dose forms such as `paracetamol 500`; that is the documented safer failure direction, but the accepted dose shapes and their tests must be explicit.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.2's Done-when explicitly requires zero false positives from unrelated nearby numbers.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: RED demonstrated in-repo first — `test_temporal_number_directly_after_a_medication_is_not_a_dose` (the peer's exact "stopped paracetamol 2 days ago" case) AND `test_a_bare_adjacent_dose_fails_toward_silence` both FAILED pre-fix with the predicted blocking `contradiction`. Fix is the allow-list direction from the leg-1 tuple: `_claims_from_tokens` now binds a dose ONLY when the number is UNIT-MARKED (next token in `_DOSE_UNITS`) — the `offset == 0` unconditional-adjacency branch is deleted; the first number found still decides either way, and the temporal deny-list alternative is recorded as rejected in the `_DOSE_WINDOW` comment because an unlisted quantity noun would fail toward FIRING, the unsafe direction. The accepted dose shapes and BOTH failure directions are pinned explicitly: the temporal case is silent, the unit-less spoken strength ("paracetamol 500") is pinned as the documented fail-toward-silence residue carried by confirmation, the unit-marked positive still fires, and severity grading (raw probability, never `uncertain`) is unchanged. Pattern siblings all unit-marked in the SAME change so the Done-when fixtures parse meaningfully on both sides instead of passing vacuously: multi-medication and marked-uncertain-dose check fixtures, the unit-marked-window test's authored side, `rule-relief`'s residue compound ("Paracetamol 500 mg / ibuprofen 400 mg discussed.") and its three matrix tests. Module docstring, parser docstring and the plan's 5.2 Done note synced to the structural rule. Verified: red → green, full suite 1095 at this fix's close, final 1102.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-003: `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py:646` — the matrix falls back to warning-code-only mock cells and its Property 2 Check-2 coverage is vacuous
- Classification: Pre-existing in Phase-5 work
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: the Validation authority explicitly requires each cell to declare canonical contents, exact source coordinates, assertion ordering, and mapped output because warning codes alone cannot detect wrong organisation or a wrong-class behaviour. It also names the multi-region/multi-medication/multi-measurement false-positive cases as part of global Property 2. The current matrix can pass while those guarantees are absent from the global oracle.
- Current behaviour: `MockCell` stores only expected error/review code tuples, and `TestMockCells` asserts only those codes. A mock can move content to a different section/order/coordinate combination that produces the same codes and pass. Separately, Property 2 iterates all `EXTRACTIVE_CELLS`, but those generated notes contain only transcript assertions (the probe counted zero authored assertions across the set), so `contradiction_warnings` has no authored comparison population; the dedicated multi-medication test lives outside the matrix property, the matrix's medication compound is an ad-hoc Axis-C test, and the claimed multi-measurement contradiction case is not exercised by Property 2.
- Desired behaviour: every non-raising matrix cell must carry and assert the full expected-output oracle appropriate to its class — canonical section contents, exact coordinates, ordering, and mapped output — and global Property 2 must range over registered clean compositions that actually enter Check 2, including authored/quoted multi-region, multi-medication, and multi-measurement controls.
- Pattern to follow: `ExtractiveCell.expected` plus `TestExtractiveCells.test_cell_matches_its_declared_output_and_codes` at `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py:352-614`; extend that declaration-driven shape rather than adding more one-off assertions. For corrupted mock outputs, declare the exact intended corruption as well as its codes.
- Pattern siblings: `MockCell`, the non-raising Axis-C cases, and Properties 1/2/5 where a warning-only or transcript-only registry can make the named global property weaker than the matrix authority.
- Invariant: a matrix cell may not pass solely because it emitted the expected warning code; the declared artifact and mapped output must also be exact, and a global property must exercise the behaviour it claims rather than an empty comparison population.
- Verification: deliberately change a mock cell's section/order/coords while preserving its warning multiset and prove the matrix fails; add registry assertions that every non-raising cell has a full output declaration; prove each Property-2 high-risk fixture contains the authored and quoted structures needed for Check 2, then assert zero errors over that registered set.
- Regression risk: expanding the oracle will expose currently implicit mock/config ordering choices and increase fixture maintenance, but all changes are test-only and directly strengthen the phase's clinical-safety evidence.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.5 and the plan's Validation / Verification section make the expected-output oracle and eight global properties the authority.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Test-only, both halves closed declaratively. (1) `MockCell` gained `expected_statements: tuple[MockStatement, ...]` — section, EXACT coordinates (derived from the DECLARED fixture turn, never provider output), exact text (deliberate corruptions written out per behaviour), speaker, and ordering — asserted for every one of the 20 mock cells alongside the code multisets AND the mapped per-clinic output derived from the declaration via `target_for`; `test_every_cell_declares_a_full_output_oracle` is the registry pin making a codes-only cell unrepresentable, and `test_the_statement_oracle_rejects_a_same_codes_corruption` is the peer's acceptance bar made permanent: a wrong-section move on faithful output provably keeps an identical code multiset and provably fails the statement oracle. (2) Property 2's population: four registered `CleanCompositionCell`s (multi-region, multi-medication, multi-measurement, negation-anchors) whose notes carry BOTH quoted and confirmed authored assertions; each checks clean per-cell (zero errors, exactly one `clinician_asserted` per authored assertion) and global Property 2 now ranges over them with a population WITNESS (`note_check._structured_authored` non-empty — imported as witness only, never as a correctness oracle, stated at the import site). Verified: corruption meta-test proves the pre-fix blindness mechanically; full suite 1102 (matrix file now 66 test instances).
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note.py:1426` — the dose structure still accepts stock counts and misses attached strengths
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: this is the SIXTH touch of the difference-not-class family and it now affects both the adversarial instrument and the shipping checker. A stock count can make `dose_change` and its independent oracle claim a genuine dosage mutation, while the checker can manufacture a blocking contradiction from that same count. In the opposite, dangerous direction, ordinary attached strengths such as `500mg` carry an explicit unit but are silently outside Check 2.
- Current behaviour: `_change_dose` and `_oracle_dose_change` both treat `medication + number + marker` as sufficient; because `tablets` is a marker, `I have paracetamol 2 tablets remaining` mutates to `4 tablets` and the named-class oracle passes. `_claims_from_tokens` makes the same stock-count text contradict `Paracetamol 1 tablet advised.`. Conversely, `content_tokens` preserves `500mg` as one token, while `_claims_from_tokens` requires the numeric token's FOLLOWING token to be in `_DOSE_UNITS`; `Paracetamol 500mg` versus `Paracetamol 1000mg` therefore emits no contradiction. The documented residue names only unit-less strength, so this explicit-unit miss is not honestly bounded.
- Desired behaviour: define a conservative dose structure that recognises attached and separated strength units while refusing inventory/count/status quantities. Keep the mock's fixture contract and its oracle independently implemented, but make both prove the medication-bound value is a dose rather than merely a medication-adjacent dose-form count.
- Pattern to follow: retain the round-21 allow-list direction. A safe bounded shape is separate/attached mass-or-volume strength (`500 mg` / `500mg`), plus dose-form counts only when an explicit regimen/action marker makes them doses; ambiguous stock/count wording must fail toward silence (checker) or loud inexpressibility (mock). Do not repair this with an ever-growing temporal/count deny-list.
- Pattern siblings: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:428` (shipping parser), `desktop/tests/test_note.py:1416` (independent named-class oracle), `_DOSE_MARKERS` / `_ORACLE_DOSE_MARKERS`, and every dose fixture in `desktop/tests/test_note_check.py` / `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py`.
- Invariant: a mock `dose_change` result must change a medication dose, never a stock, duration, date, score, or measurement; a Check-2 dose claim must be explicit enough that ordinary count/history wording cannot block, and common explicit attached strengths must not disappear into the unit-less residue.
- Verification: pin `I have paracetamol 2 tablets remaining` so the mock raises and the checker stays silent against an authored one-tablet dose; pin `Paracetamol 500mg` versus `1000mg` to fire; retain the spaced-unit positive, `2 days ago` negative, unit-less-strength residue, measurement/date loud raises, low-confidence grading, multi-medication clean case, and both matrix dose cells; run the full desktop suites.
- Regression risk: narrowing dose-form counts can silence terse true doses such as `paracetamol 2 tablets`; that is the accepted safer direction and must be stated. Adding attached-unit parsing can over-accept digit-bearing non-units unless the suffix is matched exactly against the closed strength-unit vocabulary.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Tasks 5.0 and 5.2 own the named dosage class and false-blocking contradiction boundary, and this invocation explicitly asked both directions to be attacked.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified first by token-level code trace (live `python -B` probes remain outside this leg's grant set, recorded honestly — the red tests are the executable reproductions), then RED demonstrated in-repo on all five new pins: mock mutated the stock count instead of raising, mock raised on the attached strength instead of mutating, checker fired the count contradiction, checker missed the attached-unit conflict, AND checker fired on the mg-vs-g restatement (a bonus pre-fix false positive the unit-blind value comparison hid). Fix, both sides, allow-list direction: CHECKER — `_STRENGTH_UNITS` (canonical map, mass/volume only; count words removed) replaces `_DOSE_UNITS`; `_dose_quantity` full-match-parses bare numbers AND attached `<number><strength-suffix>` tokens (unrecognised suffixes like `20min` are nothing); the canonical unit joins the ANCHOR so cross-unit pairs are UNMATCHED (silence, no unit conversion claimed); a digit-bearing unrecognised token refuses-and-breaks (first-quantity-decides preserved). MOCK — `_DOSE_MARKERS` loses the count words, `_ATTACHED_STRENGTH_RE` accepts attached strengths as dose sites, `_MUTATION_REQUIREMENT` reworded; ORACLE — independent `_ORACLE_ATTACHED_STRENGTH_RE` (same-unit both sides, numbers differ) or marker-following, counts removed from the oracle vocabulary. The bounded residue is now COMPLETE in the `_DOSE_WINDOW` comment and module docstring: unit-less strengths, count/dose-form quantities (prescribed and stock mechanically inseparable), temporal/measurement quantities, cross-unit restatements, spelled quantities — every form pinned by a silence test, both deny-list repairs recorded as rejected (fail-toward-firing). Siblings retained green: spaced-unit positive, `2 days ago` negative, measurement/date raises, low-confidence grading, multi-med clean case, both matrix dose cells, no-op-scan fixture moved to strength form (`codeine 30 mg`). Verified red → green; suites 1107 at this fix's close, final 1108.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-002: `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py:1428` — two of four Property-2 cells still have zero matched Check-2 comparisons
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: Round 21's safety-matrix gap recurs by a different route. The new witness proves only that SOME authored structure parsed; it does not prove an authored claim shared an anchor with quoted evidence, which is the population `contradiction_warnings` actually compares. The global zero-false-positive property can therefore stay green without exercising Check 2 for the named cell.
- Current behaviour: both `TestCleanCompositionCells` and global Property 2 assert only `_structured_authored(note)`. Direct population measurement found matched claim-key counts of 2 (`clean-multi-region`), 2 (`clean-multi-medication`), 0 (`clean-multi-measurement`), and 0 (`clean-negation-anchors`). The measurement fixture's quoted side parses no structured claim; the negation fixture parses `numbness` while its authored side parses `dizziness`, so their key intersection is empty. These newly registered non-raising clean cells also have no full expected-statement/mapped-output assertion of their own; the registry pin at `test_every_cell_declares_a_full_output_oracle` covers only `MOCK_CELLS`.
- Desired behaviour: every registered Property-2 clean cell must contain at least one same-valued matched authored/quoted claim pair, while retaining the extra regions, medications, measurements, or symptoms that challenge false-positive isolation. Each non-raising clean cell should also assert its complete declared statement sequence and mapped output, so the expected-output-oracle rule has no auxiliary-cell exemption.
- Pattern to follow: use the checker's private parsers only as a POPULATION witness (as the existing comment permits), but witness the actual authored/quoted key intersection rather than authored existence. Keep correctness independent through cell-side declarations and exact warning/output expectations.
- Pattern siblings: `desktop/tests/test_note_matrix.py:1129` / `:1135` (the two zero-match fixtures), `:1170` (per-cell weak witness), `:1406–1428` (global Property 2), and `CleanCompositionCell` / `_run_clean` (missing full output declaration/assertion).
- Invariant: a global property must exercise the comparison relation it names, not merely one input collection; every non-raising matrix cell must be checked against a full, independently declared artifact oracle as well as warning codes.
- Verification: make a clean cell with authored structure but no shared quoted key fail the population witness; assert a non-zero matched-key count for all four cells; retain zero errors and exact `clinician_asserted` counts; deliberately corrupt section/order/coords in a clean cell and prove its full oracle fails; rerun all eight property tests and the full desktop suites.
- Regression risk: adding a shared anchor carelessly can turn a clean fixture into a duplicate of a unit test or introduce a real contradiction. Declare the matched value and unrelated distractors explicitly so the fixture still tests isolation rather than merely parser reachability.

#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:452` — a separated strength unit does not participate in contradiction confidence
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: `contradiction` is error-severity and blocks write, copy, and Complete, while the low-confidence variant is review-only. For a separated dose, the unit word is the evidence that turns a bare number into dose structure and selects the unit-scoped anchor; excluding its confidence lets uncertain transcript evidence create a false hard block.
- Current behaviour: lines 448–450 resolve the separated unit from the token after the numeric candidate, but line 452 calls `_claim_evidence(segment_index, (entry, candidate))` with only medication and number. With high-confidence `paracetamol 250` and low-confidence `mg`, the parser therefore reports an error-level 250-vs-500 contradiction and source coordinates ending at the number. Attached units do not have this asymmetry because number and unit occupy the same candidate token.
- Desired behaviour: every transcript token required to establish the structured dose claim — medication, quantity, and a separated unit when present — must contribute to `min_probability` and the warning's evidence coordinates. A low-confidence separated unit must grade the mismatch `contradiction_low_confidence`; the same text with all three tokens high-confidence must remain `contradiction`.
- Pattern to follow: build the `involved` tuple at the same branch that proves the dose shape, adding the resolved following-unit token only for separated units; keep attached units represented by their existing single candidate token.
- Pattern siblings: `_claims_from_tokens`' attached-unit branch; `test_note_check.py`'s high-probability-number and low-confidence contradiction tests; `test_note_matrix.py:1464` currently lowers the number rather than the separated unit.
- Invariant: every source token whose identity is necessary to construct a transcript-side claim key or value participates in confidence grading and evidence coordinates.
- Verification: add a red/green separated-unit case with medication and number above threshold and only the unit below it; assert review severity and coordinates spanning through the unit. Retain attached-unit, all-high-confidence separated-unit, and high-probability-`uncertain` number pins.
- Regression risk: warning coordinates for separated-unit contradictions widen by one word, and any caller/rendering test that asserted the old truncated interval must move with the semantic fix; attached-unit behaviour must not change.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.2 explicitly owns raw-probability grading of transcript-side contradiction evidence.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (involved tuple was `(entry, candidate)` — the separated unit token resolved the anchor but contributed neither probability nor coordinates), then RED demonstrated: `test_a_low_confidence_separated_unit_grades_to_review` (med+number 0.9, `mg` 0.2, identical statement contexts) produced a hard `contradiction` pre-fix. Fix per the finding's Pattern-to-follow, folded into the round's parser restructure: `_dose_claims` builds `involved = (medication, *atom_tokens)` at the same point that proves the dose shape — for separated atoms that includes the unit token, for attached atoms the single candidate token as before — so `min_probability` and the evidence coordinates now span every token the claim's identity rests on (the invariant, verbatim). Green: the low-confidence-unit case grades `contradiction_low_confidence` review with coords `(0,0,2)` spanning through the unit; the all-high-confidence same-state twin stays `contradiction` error; the attached-unit and high-probability-`uncertain`-number pins retained (the latter refit to an identical-context fixture per PR-MED-002's severity contract, uncertain-flag assertion preserved). Verified red → green; suite green at leg close (1117).
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-002: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:584` — dose keys conflate medication history, inventory strength, and an intentional dose change
- Classification: Pre-existing in Phase-5 work
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: a matched medication+unit anchor does not establish that two quantities describe the same clinical state. Dose reduction or titration is ordinary clinical documentation, yet the emitted error is not acknowledgeable: the clinician must retract or regenerate, which can force a legitimate medication change out of the note. The same gap turns a product-strength inventory statement into a false blocker.
- Current behaviour: `_StructuredClaim` carries only type, anchor and value; a dose anchor is exactly `<medication> <canonical-unit>` at lines 454–458. The loops at lines 584–594 compare every authored claim with every quoted claim without section, temporal status, action, or intent. Consequently quoted `I take paracetamol 500 mg each morning` plus authored `Reduce paracetamol to 250 mg twice daily.` emits blocking `contradiction`, although both statements are simultaneously true. Likewise `I have paracetamol 500 mg tablets remaining` conflicts with authored 250 mg: removing count words from the unit vocabulary excludes `2 tablets` as the quantity, but does not exclude a strength followed by count/status wording.
- Desired behaviour: an error-level dose contradiction must require mechanically established identity of the medication fact, including its clinical state/time or equivalent structured context. When the checker cannot prove that identity, a differing strength must fail toward review or silence rather than a hard contradiction.
- Pattern to follow: extend the structured claim key with an explicit, conservatively derived context/status dimension or narrow error-level comparison to a mechanically unambiguous same-state shape; do not add a growing temporal/status deny-list, whose unlisted wording would again fail toward blocking.
- Pattern siblings: authored-vs-authored comparisons have the same problem for staged plan assertions; the stock-strength probe and ordinary current-dose-to-new-dose phrasing should be fixed by the same semantic boundary, not special-cased separately.
- Invariant: `contradiction` may block only two logically incompatible claims about the same medication, unit, and clinical state — sharing medication and unit alone is insufficient.
- Verification: pin current-dose history plus an explicit reduction/titration as non-error; pin product-strength inventory/status wording as non-error; add an authored-authored staged-change case; retain a genuinely same-state 250-vs-500 control that still fires at the intended severity.
- Regression risk: a conservative context requirement reduces automatic dose-conflict coverage. Preserve a narrow, explicitly declared error population and route ambiguous cases toward review/silence, consistent with the checker's documented safer direction.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.2 and this invocation explicitly require false-blocking legitimate clinical phrasing to be tested.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (claims keyed on `<medication> <unit>` compared with no state dimension), then RED demonstrated on all three false-blockers: current-dose history vs explicit reduction, product-strength inventory vs authored dose, and an authored-authored staged/split regimen each emitted blocking `contradiction` pre-fix. Fix per the finding's Pattern-to-follow (extend the claim with a conservatively derived context, no deny-list): `_StructuredClaim` gained a dose-only `context` — the assertion's full token sequence with the strength atom collapsed to one placeholder (attached and separated spellings collapse identically) — and `_same_state` requires token-IDENTICAL contexts before the `error` grade: the mechanically provable "same medication fact with incompatible values". Every other matched-anchor difference grades the NEW registered review code `dose_mismatch` (`note.NOTE_WARNING_SEVERITY` extended, nothing re-graded): surfaced beside the assertion, acknowledgeable, never blocks — dose changes, titrations, inventory strengths and history phrasing are ordinary documentation. The narrow error population is explicitly declared and pinned by the retained control (`test_a_same_state_dose_conflict_still_blocks`: "Paracetamol 500 mg advised." vs "Paracetamol 250 mg advised." still hard-blocks; the quoted/authored identical-context twin ditto). Green: reduce/inventory/staged cases all `dose_mismatch` review; the three sibling tests whose fixtures were cross-context (window-binding, residue compound in both suites) refit to expect review with docstrings recording why; laterality/negation error grading deliberately untouched (their staged-change analog is not a recorded finding — named residue, not smoothed). No must-pause was needed: the error-level safety control is preserved for the provable population and nothing is silenced — ambiguity lands at review. Verified red → green; suite 1117.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-003: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:437` — common explicit-strength word orders remain silent outside the medication-first window
- Classification: Same-family
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: round 22's dangerous-direction fix claims that an attached or separated explicit strength participates in dose comparison, but the implementation recognises only a medication token followed by a quantity within three tokens. Common, fully structured statements can therefore disagree on a dose and pass with no warning.
- Current behaviour: the dose loop starts only when the current token is a medication and scans the next `_DOSE_WINDOW` tokens. It cannot represent an explicit strength before its medication (`250 mg of paracetamol`) and it stops before an explicit strength in `paracetamol at a dose of 250 mg`. Both forms carry medication, numeric quantity, and canonical strength unit, yet direct probes against authored 500 mg returned no contradiction. The `_DOSE_WINDOW` comment calls its listed residue complete but names neither inverse order nor explicit strengths beyond the three-token forward grammar, and no silence or positive test pins either boundary.
- Desired behaviour: recognise conservative, mechanically explicit strength structures in the ordinary inverse and dose-phrase word orders, or — if the practitioner deliberately accepts their silence — name these structural forms in the bounded residue and pin them. Given that both examples contain the full medication+quantity+strength relation without conversion or semantic guessing, the preferred fix is to compare them.
- Pattern to follow: parse the local numeric-strength atom first (attached or separated), then bind it to a medication through a finite allow-list of explicit grammatical relations such as `<medication> ... <quantity><unit>` and `<quantity><unit> of <medication>`; do not widen the positional window without retaining strength and relation structure.
- Pattern siblings: `_dose_quantity`, the attached/separated positive tests in `test_note_check.py`, the first-quantity-decides rule, and the matrix dose fixtures. Keep temporal numbers, measurements, counts, cross-unit pairs, and unit-less quantities silent.
- Invariant: a common word-order variation must not erase an otherwise explicit same-unit medication-strength discrepancy, and new grammar must not re-admit proximity-only numbers.
- Verification: add red/green contradictions for `250 mg of paracetamol` and `paracetamol at a dose of 250 mg` against authored 500 mg, plus structurally similar temporal/count/measurement negatives; rerun all residue pins and the Property-2 matched-population cells.
- Regression risk: broader relation matching can recreate rounds 19–22's false positives if it becomes a window search. Keep the accepted grammar closed and exact, and prove every new branch with both a positive and its nearest non-dose negative.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — this is the same attached/separated explicit-strength class round 22 intended to close, in the dangerous false-negative direction.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (the loop started only at medication tokens scanning forward, so `250 mg of paracetamol` and `paracetamol at a dose of 250 mg` never produced a claim), then RED demonstrated: both probes emitted NOTHING pre-fix. Fix per the finding's Pattern-to-follow — atom-first, closed relations, never a widened window: `_strength_atoms` finds explicit number+unit atoms (attached or separated) anywhere in the token stream, and `_bound_medication` binds each through EXACTLY three relations — R1 medication-first (the rounds-19-22 grammar with first-quantity-decides digit hygiene preserved), R2 `<atom> of <medication>` exact adjacency, R3 the literal phrase `dose of` immediately before the atom with the medication within the bounded non-digit gap. An atom bound through no relation is silent, which makes the residue COMPLETE-BY-CONSTRUCTION (silent forms = quantities that never become a bound atom), rewritten in the `_DOSE_WINDOW` comment and module docstring per the recurring claim-outruns-structure class. Green: both word orders now surface — as `dose_mismatch` review, the honest severity under PR-MED-002's same-state contract since their statement contexts differ from the authored wording (surfaced, never silent; identical-context conflicts still error) — and every new branch carries its nearest non-dose negative (`2 of the paracetamol tablets`, `20 minutes of the track`, both pinned silent). All rounds-19-22 silence pins, the Property-2 matched-population cells, and both matrix dose cells retained green. Verified red → green; suites at leg close: ruff clean, mypy strict 28 files, pytest **1117 passed**.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-001: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:483` — medication-first binding shadows the exact inverse relation and can bind an explicit strength to the wrong entity
- Classification: Fix-induced
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: the round-23 false-negative fix is not closed under ordinary multi-medication wording. A preceding medication inside R1's broad window steals an atom that has the exact R2 shape `<atom> of <medication>`, so a real paracetamol discrepancy can again be silent; the same broad R1 also treats a nearby explicit volume such as water as the preceding medication's dose and can manufacture a false blocker.
- Current behaviour: `_bound_medication` evaluates R1 first and returns immediately on the first preceding lexicon medication with no intervening digit. R2 is considered only afterward. The probe `ibuprofen with 250 mg of paracetamol advised` therefore yields `ibuprofen mg=250`, not `paracetamol mg=250`; an authored paracetamol 500 mg claim has no matched anchor and produces no warning. Separately, `paracetamol mixed with 5 ml water` satisfies R1 and yields `paracetamol ml=5`, despite the atom describing the mixing volume rather than medication strength.
- Desired behaviour: binding must be unambiguous and relation-specific. An exact local relation (R2 `<atom> of <medication>` and the literal R3 `dose of`) must not be shadowed by an ambient R1 candidate; ambiguous competing candidates must fail toward silence/review, and R1 must not claim an explicit measurement merely because it lies within the non-digit window.
- Pattern to follow: resolve relation candidates before returning — prefer the exact grammar that contains both atom and medication, or reject competing bindings — then narrow R1 to a finite medication-first grammar whose connectors are part of the relation rather than treating every non-digit gap as equivalent.
- Pattern siblings: `desktop/tests/test_note_check.py:542` (inverse positive lacks a preceding medication), `:375` (multi-medication clean case uses medication-first order only), and `:392` (measurement clean case contains no medication). These three fixtures do not exercise the cross-product that exposes the mis-binding.
- Invariant: one strength atom may bind to at most one mechanically unambiguous medication, and a more explicit relation must never lose to a weaker positional candidate.
- Verification: add a red/green multi-medication inverse case (`ibuprofen with 250 mg of paracetamol` versus authored paracetamol 500 mg) that must surface on the paracetamol anchor, plus a medication-adjacent non-dose strength-unit measurement (`paracetamol mixed with 5 ml water`) that must not hard-block. Retain all three existing positive relations and the rounds-19–23 silence pins.
- Regression risk: changing precedence can move anchors in multi-medication statements; narrowing R1 can silence valid free-word-order doses. Keep the three accepted relations explicit and add paired positive/negative fixtures for every permitted connector.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — Task 5.2 and this confirmation round explicitly require tracing all three relations for mis-binding and preventing recurrence of the rounds-19–22 false-positive/false-negative classes.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (R1 returned before R2 was consulted; R1's gap admitted ANY non-digit word), then RED demonstrated: `ibuprofen with 250 mg of paracetamol` surfaced NOTHING against authored paracetamol 500 mg (the atom bound to ibuprofen), and `paracetamol mixed with 5 ml water` emitted a dose warning against an authored volume. Fix per the finding's Pattern-to-follow, a NARROWING not a broadening: `_dose_binding` COLLECTS candidates across all three relations before deciding — exact-inverse R2 first, dose-phrase R3, then medication-first R1 — and two relations naming DIFFERENT medications are ambiguous and bind NOTHING (silence, never a guessed anchor: the invariant verbatim). R1 and R3's gaps are no longer "any non-digit tokens": `_connector_walk` accepts only the closed `_DOSE_CONNECTORS` set (`at`, `to`, `a`, `the`), so `mixed`/`with` break the relation and the mixing volume is silent; `_digit_bearing_between` became dead code and was REMOVED (first-quantity-decides is now implied by connector closure — digit-bearing gap tokens are not connectors). Green: the multi-medication inverse surfaces on the PARACETAMOL anchor; the mixing volume is silent; every rounds-19–23 positive relation, silence pin, clean cell and matrix fixture retained green (the multi-med compound and `Reduce … to 250 mg` traces re-verified under the narrowed connectors). Residue updated complete-by-construction: non-connector gaps and ambiguous bindings are named silent forms. Verified red → green; final suite 1123.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-002: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:524` — low-confidence `of` / `dose of` relation words can still create an error-grade dose contradiction
- Classification: Fix-induced
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: R2 and R3 exist only because literal relation words bind the atom to a medication. If one of those words is acoustically uncertain, the checker has uncertain evidence for the claim identity; ignoring that uncertainty can turn a dubious parse into an unacknowledgeable hard block, the same safety/severity defect round 23 fixed for a separated unit.
- Current behaviour: `_dose_claims` passes only `(medication, *atom_tokens)` to `_claim_evidence`. R2's required `of` and R3's required `dose of` tokens are absent. In the inverse probe with medication/number/unit at 0.9 and only `of` at 0.2, the claim retained `min_probability=0.9`; an otherwise identical 250-vs-500 pair therefore routes through `_same_state` to error-grade `contradiction`, not `contradiction_low_confidence`. The coordinate interval happens to span the relation word because it lies between atom and medication, but its probability is not considered.
- Desired behaviour: every transcript token whose literal identity is required to select a dose-binding relation must participate in confidence grading. At minimum R2 must include `of`, and R3 must include both `dose` and `of`; the implementation should make the involved-token set a result of the chosen relation so future relation additions cannot omit their evidence.
- Pattern to follow: have `_bound_medication` return a binding object (medication index plus relation kind/evidence indices) rather than only an index, and derive `_claim_evidence` from that complete binding plus the atom.
- Pattern siblings: round-23 PR-MED-001's separated-unit invariant; `desktop/tests/test_note_check.py:663` covers a low-confidence unit but no relation token; `:542` and `:560` cover R2/R3 only at default high confidence.
- Invariant: every source token necessary to establish a transcript-side claim's anchor, value, or binding relation contributes to `min_probability` and evidence coordinates.
- Verification: add same-context R2 and R3 conflicts with only `of`, then only `dose`, below `UNCERTAINTY_THRESHOLD`; each must grade `contradiction_low_confidence` review and point across the complete relation. Retain high-confidence twins as error and the separated-unit regression.
- Regression risk: widening the evidence set can demote a real conflict when a connector is low-confidence. That is the intended conservative direction; medication/atom confidence and all-high-confidence error controls must remain pinned.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — this is the direct new-relation sibling of round-23 PR-MED-001 and Task 5.2's raw-probability severity contract.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (`_dose_claims` passed only medication+atom to `_claim_evidence`), then RED demonstrated: same-context R2 and R3 conflicts with only `of` (resp. only `dose`) at 0.2 both produced hard `contradiction` instead of the review grade. Fix exactly as the finding's Pattern-to-follow prescribes: `_bound_medication` became `_dose_binding` returning a BINDING OBJECT — medication index plus the indices of every relation token whose literal identity established the binding (connectors, `of`, `dose`) — and `_dose_claims` derives `_claim_evidence` from the COMPLETE binding (medication + relation evidence + atom tokens), so the involved-token set is a RESULT of the chosen relation and a future relation cannot omit its evidence (the finding's future-proofing clause, satisfied structurally). Green: low-confidence `of` grades `contradiction_low_confidence` with coordinates spanning the full relation (0,0,3); low-confidence `dose` likewise; the high-confidence R2/R3 twins stay error; the round-23 separated-unit regression and the all-high-confidence controls retained. The intended conservative direction is documented: a low-confidence connector now demotes a real conflict to review — acknowledged in the finding's own Regression-risk line as intended. Verified red → green; final suite 1123.
- /fix date: 2026-08-11
- /fix applied by: Claude Code

#### PR-MED-003: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/note_check.py:692` — token-identical context is not sufficient to make inventory strengths mutually exclusive
- Classification: Fix-induced
- Triage: Fix-now
- Fix route: premium-only
- Why it matters: the stated severity contract says inventory strengths never hard-block, yet a patient can legitimately have tablets of two strengths at home. Treating two otherwise identical inventory statements as logically incompatible creates an error that blocks write, copy, and Complete and can force legitimate medication information out of the note.
- Current behaviour: `_same_state` equates any two dose contexts that become token-identical after the atom is collapsed. The probes `I have paracetamol 500 mg tablets remaining` and `I have paracetamol 250 mg tablets remaining` both collapse to `i have paracetamol <dose> tablets remaining`, so `_same_state` returns true and the comparison loops emit blocking `contradiction`. The existing inventory regression compares inventory wording with `Paracetamol 250 mg advised.`; it proves only the different-context path to `dose_mismatch`, not that inventory strengths themselves never block.
- Desired behaviour: error-grade identity must prove an exclusive medication-dose slot, not merely textual sameness after removing the value. Inventory/product-strength membership and other multi-valued contexts must grade `dose_mismatch` review even when their surrounding tokens are identical; semantically equivalent but mechanically unproved current-dose paraphrases may remain review by the declared conservative boundary.
- Pattern to follow: define a closed positive grammar for the narrow error population (an explicitly current administration/regimen fact with one exclusive strength slot), or carry a structured relation/context class whose inventory/product-strength form is never error-grade. Do not repair this with an open-ended status deny-list.
- Pattern siblings: `desktop/tests/test_note_check.py:612` (inventory versus advised dose only), `:630` (staged wording differs), and `:646` (the retained hard-error control uses `advised` but does not prove that the surrounding context is an exclusive state).
- Invariant: `contradiction` may block only claims that cannot both be true; token identity after value collapse is necessary but not sufficient for multi-valued inventory/product facts.
- Verification: add quoted/authored and authored/authored pairs for two simultaneously held paracetamol tablet strengths with otherwise identical wording; both must emit `dose_mismatch`, never `contradiction`. Replace or supplement the hard-error control with a fixture whose closed grammar actually establishes one current exclusive dose slot, and retain reduction/staged-regimen review cases.
- Regression risk: narrowing the error population can downgrade genuine dose conflicts. Keep every differing matched anchor surfaced as `dose_mismatch` and pin the exact positive grammar chosen for the remaining hard-error population.
- Scope-expansion disposition: In scope — this is the round-23 severity contract's explicit inventory-strength promise and the invocation specifically asks whether legitimate inventory can still hard-block.
- /fix decision: Applied
- /fix notes: Verified by trace (`_same_state` was bare context token-identity, so twin inventory statements hard-blocked), then RED demonstrated: `I have paracetamol 500 mg tablets remaining` vs the 250 mg twin emitted blocking `contradiction` in both quoted/authored and authored/authored pairings. Fix per the finding's Pattern-to-follow — a CLOSED POSITIVE grammar for the error population, no status deny-list: `_same_state` now requires identical contexts AND `_exclusive_context` — every context token drawn from the closed `_EXCLUSIVE_DOSE_CONTEXT` allow-list (administration verbs, frequency/timing words, dose-relation words) plus medication names — i.e. the wording of an explicitly current regimen fact with ONE strength slot, the only shape whose differing values cannot both be true. Inventory wording (`have`/`tablets`/`remaining`) and ANY novel/unlisted wording fall outside the grammar and grade `dose_mismatch` review even when token-identical — the allow-list direction, so unlisted wording fails toward review, never toward blocking (the invariant: token identity is necessary but not sufficient). Green: both inventory pairings grade review; the hard-error population is pinned POSITIVELY (`test_the_exclusive_grammar_error_population_is_pinned`: `I take paracetamol N mg twice daily`, plus the retained `advised` and timing-phrase controls, all re-verified inside the grammar); reduction/staged-regimen review cases retained. Named residue, per the finding's own allowance: semantically-current paraphrases OUTSIDE the closed grammar stay review — the declared conservative boundary. Verification note, recorded honestly: the first green run's ONE failure was `test_recorder_no_sockets_during_real_whisper_transcription` — an untouched live-ML integration test whose child hit its 300 s transcription deadline during a machine-load-degraded 40 min suite run (all 1122 others green, every note suite green); a clean rerun passed 1123/1123 in 64 s, confirming load-flake, not a regression — no note-pipeline code is in that test's path.
